In addition, to support the training and evaluation of wildlife individual-consistent I2V, we construct WildlifeVid, a wildlife-centric video dataset with high-quality, temporally coherent clips and individual-level identity labels. Experiments on I2V generation and downstream animal re-identification (ReID) show that WildIcon achieves stronger individual consistency than existing baselines, and that its filtered outputs can serve as useful candidate training augmentations for downstream ReID.

</details>


### [138] [EchoDino: A pediatric foundation model for transferable echocardiographic analysis across the lifespan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05603)
*Sheng Cheng,Donnchadh M. O'Sullivan,Daniel J. Penny,Craig G. Rusin,Minh B. Nguyen,Devika Subramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoDino：基于DINOv3的自监督儿科超声心动图基础模型，用170万未标注视频训练，通过冻结编码器与MEMS采样在多项儿科和成人任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图解读需整合全局解剖、局部结构与动态运动，而现有机器学习模型多为单一用途且依赖昂贵标注数据，儿科因数据稀缺和随年龄变化的解剖结构面临更大障碍。

Method: 将DINOv3框架适配到370万帧、来自170万个未标注儿科超声心动图视频进行自监督预训练；提出运动偏置熵最大化采样（MEMS）选取视频级分析中最具信息量的帧；冻结编码器提取兼具全局、局部与密集空间细节的表征。

Result: 在九个儿科与成人数据集上，视窗分类准确率由0.609提升至0.889，结构性心脏病检测AUC由0.811提升至0.872；年龄估计误差由3.857降至1.389年；取得最佳分割精度并降低射血分数误差。

Conclusion: 通过从无标注儿科数据泛化到成人超声心动图，EchoDino为覆盖全生命周期的心脏图像分析提供了通用基础模型。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the most widely used cardiac imaging modality, yet interpretation demands integrating visual evidence across global anatomy, localized structures and dynamic cardiac motion. Machine-learning models have automated individual tasks, but they are typically built for a single purpose and depend on expensively labeled datasets - a barrier particularly acute in pediatric care, where data are scarce and anatomy changes with age. Here we present EchoDino, a self-supervised foundation model for echocardiography, created by adapting the DINOv3 framework to 3.7 million frames from 1.7 million unlabeled pediatric echocardiography videos. With its encoder frozen, EchoDino produces representations that capture global context, local anatomy, and dense spatial detail. We introduce Motion-biased Entropy Maximization Sampling (MEMS) to select the most informative frames for video-level analysis. Across nine pediatric and adult datasets, EchoDino outperformed strong baseline models, raising view-classification accuracy from 0.609 to 0.889 and the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve for structural-heart-disease detection from 0.811 to 0.872, while also cutting age-estimation error from 3.857 to 1.389 years, achieving the best segmentation accuracy and lowering ejection-fraction errors. By generalizing from label-free pediatric data to adult echocardiography, EchoDino offers a versatile foundation for cardiac image analysis across the lifespan.

</details>


### [139] [Kandinsky 6.0 Video: Foundation Models for Synchronized Video and Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05608)
*Team Kandinsky,Julia Agafonova,Bulat Akhmatov,Mikhail Aksyutin,Grigorii Alekseenko,Anastasia Aliaskina,Olga Androsova,Vladimir Arkhipkin,Anna Averchenkova,Alexander Belykh,Serafima Bocharova,Sofiya Bogakovskaya,Anton Bukashkin,Mark Bulygin,Kirill Buzygin,Irina Cheremnykh,Kirill Chernyshev,Mikhail Chernyshov,Vladimir Chernyy,David Chikovani,Georgy Daniltsev,Denis Dimitrov,Anna Dmitrienko,Vladimir Dokholyan,Sergey Emelyanov,Dmitry Ermilov,Georgii Fedorov,Polina Gavrilova,Nikolai Gerasimenko,Aleksandr Gordeev,Andrey Inozemtsev,Andrei Ivaniuta,Alexander Ivanov,Mikhail Karaev,Anastasiia Kargapoltseva,Ivan Kirillov,Nikita Kiselev,Valeria Kobenko,Yury Kolabushin,Denis Koposov,Anatoly Korobov,Vladimir Korviakov,Kirill Kozlov,Denis Krzhivokolskiy,Konstantin Kuklev,Alexander Kunitsyn,Sergey Kuzin,Vladislav Lakhtionov,Alexey Letunovskiy,Maxim Litvinov,Alexander Lyulkov,Georgy Makarov,Kirill Malakhov,Egor Malykh,Mikhail Mamaev,Dmitrii Mikhailov,Polina Mikhailova,Ivan Mikheev,Elizaveta Muromtseva,Nikolai Nazarkin,Tatiana Nikulina,Lev Novitskiy,Stanislav Onuchin,Nikita Osterov,Denis Parkhomenko,Anatoliy Parpara,Vladimir Polovnikov,Konstantin Reznikov,Azat Saginbaev,Nikita Samsonov,Alexander Sentsov,Nikita Shaimov,Artem Sherstyuk,Andrey Shutkin,Egor Silvestrov,Bulat Suleimanov,Matvey Suprunov,Sergey Taranov,Irina Tolstykh,Tatiana Trofimuk,Ilya Trushkin,Aleksandra Tsybina,Olga Varlashina,Viacheslav Vasilev,Ilya Vasiliev,Eugeny Vilisov,Sergey Yakubson,Konstantin Zakharov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Kandinsky 6.0 Video系列音视频同步生成基础模型，包含Lite（3B）与Pro（29B）两版本，可生成5秒44kHz同步音频视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速多模态（音频-视频）生成领域的开放研究与部署，提升文本/图像到音视频同步生成的质量与可及性。

Method: 基于Kandinsky 5.0视频能力，采用双流CrossDiT架构，通过双向交叉注意力连接预训练视频流与新训练音频流，实现时序与语义对齐；采用连续预训练策略（先音频流从零训练，再联合训练），随后进行监督微调、基于强化学习的后训练与蒸馏；内置超分模型将分辨率提升至Full-HD。

Result: 在人工并排评测中，Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro明显优于前代Kandinsky 5.0 Video Pro，与领先音视频生成模型相比具竞争力，尤其在语音质量方面表现突出。

Conclusion: Kandinsky 6.0 Video在音视频同步生成上取得显著进展，并以MIT许可开源代码、模型权重及diffusers集成，推动多媒体生成领域的研究与部署。

Abstract: We present Kandinsky 6.0 Video, a family of foundation diffusion models for synchronized text-to-audio-video generation, comprising Kandinsky 6.0 Video Lite (3B parameters) and Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro (29B parameters). Both models generate 5-second video clips with synchronized 44 kHz audio, including lip-sync, in text-to-audio-video (T2AV) and image-to-audio-video (I2AV) modes; a built-in super-resolution model raises the output resolution to Full-HD (1920$\times$1080). Building on the video generation capabilities of Kandinsky 5.0, Kandinsky 6.0 Video employs a dual-stream CrossDiT architecture that connects a pretrained video stream and a newly trained audio stream through bidirectional cross-attention for temporal and semantic alignment. Our continuous pretraining strategy first trains the audio stream from scratch on large-scale audio corpora and then trains both streams jointly on paired audio-video data while preserving unimodal fidelity; pretraining is followed by supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement-learning-based post-training, and distillation. In side-by-side human evaluation, Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro clearly outperforms its predecessor, Kandinsky 5.0 Video Pro, and remains competitive with leading audio-video generation models, particularly in speech quality. To accelerate open research and deployment in multimedia generation, we release the code, model checkpoints, and diffusers integration under the MIT license.

</details>


### [140] [StageVLN: Spatial and Trajectory Auxiliary Guidance for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05664)
*Anh Dao,Quan-Dung Pham,Le Danh Vinh,The Anh Nguyen,Nguyen Viet Tri Pham,Yiyu Chen,Pham Tuyen Le,Van-Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StageVLN 通过在训练阶段引入冻结几何基础模型与航向/路径进度监督来塑造导航表示，部署时移除所有辅助组件，在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 上以 4B 骨干取得领先性能且推理无额外几何编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN 策略受益于大型视觉语言模型提供的强语义先验，但标准动作监督不会显式促使中间表示保留场景几何、相对朝向或全局回合进度。引入深度估计器、显式地图、点云或几何基础模型虽能提供此类结构信息，却带来额外计算、内存开销与部署时的架构依赖。

Method: 提出 StageVLN 训练框架：用冻结的几何基础模型向分层导航器状态提供多层级空间引导，并以相对朝向目标与专家路径进度目标提供互补的轨迹状态监督；所有辅助组件仅在训练时使用，部署时移除，保持原始推理通路。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 验证未见集上，使用 4B 参数骨干达到 56.3% SR 与 51.4% SPL，推理时无需额外几何编码器；在 RxR-CE 上达到 54.3% SR，且不依赖额外导航训练数据或推理期几何编码器。

Conclusion: 通过特权空间与轨迹引导进行训练期表示塑形，可在不改变推理架构的前提下提升 VLN 性能，兼顾精度与部署效率。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) policies increasingly benefit from strong semantic priors provided by large vision-language models (VLMs). However, standard action supervision does not explicitly encourage intermediate representations to preserve scene geometry, relative orientation, or global episode progress. Incorporating depth estimators, explicit maps, point clouds, or geometry foundation models at inference can provide such structure but introduces additional computation, memory overhead, and architectural dependence during deployment. We introduce StageVLN, a training framework that shapes navigation representations through privileged spatial and trajectory guidance while preserving the original inference pathway. A frozen geometry foundation model provides multi-level spatial guidance to hierarchical navigator states, while relative-heading and expert-route progress objectives provide complementary trajectory-state supervision. All auxiliary components are used only during training and removed at deployment. On R2R-CE validation-unseen, StageVLN achieves 56.3\% SR and 51.4\% SPL with a 4B-parameter backbone, without an additional geometry encoder at inference. On RxR-CE, it achieves 54.3\% SR without additional navigation training data or a geometry encoder at inference.

</details>


### [141] [Difference Feature Map Distillation: Transferring Inter-Sample Relational Knowledge Towards Efficient Transformer-Based Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05707)
*Zhicheng Ding,Xinyu Chu,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DFM-KD（差异特征图知识蒸馏），一种面向Transformer视觉目标跟踪的关系型蒸馏框架，通过迁移样本间特征差异而非绝对激活的相似性，提升学生模型的鲁棒性与跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知中的视觉目标跟踪系统需在严格时延与功耗约束下保持鲁棒。基于Transformer的跟踪器虽精度领先，但计算与内存开销大，难以部署在实时、资源受限平台上。传统特征蒸馏方法通过逐点误差（如MSE）对齐师生特征，忽视特征空间的关系结构。

Method: 提出DFM-KD关系型蒸馏框架，专注于Transformer跟踪器。不同于最小化师生特征逐点差异的传统方法，DFM-KD借助样本间特征差异（inter-sample feature differences）传递知识，显式对齐特征空间的关系结构；蒸馏教师建模外观变化与样本间一致性的方式，而非强制绝对激活相似。

Result: 大量实验表明，DFM-KD在跟踪精度（precision）和成功率（success rate）上均持续优于传统特征级蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 通过迁移样本间关系结构，DFM-KD使蒸馏模型具备更强的特征鲁棒性与更优的跟踪性能，为在资源受限平台上部署Transformer跟踪器提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In autonomous driving perception, visual object tracking systems must satisfy stringent latency and power constraints while remaining robust in complex and dynamic environments. Although transformer-based trackers achieve state-of-the-art accuracy, their substantial computational and memory overheads hinder deployment on real-time, resource-constrained platforms. To move toward this goal, we propose Difference Feature Map Knowledge Distillation (DFM-KD), a novel relational distillation framework tailored for transformer-based visual object tracking. Unlike conventional feature distillation methods that minimize point-wise discrepancies (e.g., mean squared error) between teacher and student feature representations, DFM-KD transfers knowledge through inter-sample feature differences, explicitly aligning the relational structure of the feature space. By distilling how the teacher models appearance variation and consistency across samples, rather than enforcing similarity in absolute activations, DFM-KD enables the student to better capture the structural dynamics of visual changes within a batch. As a result, the distilled model exhibits enhanced feature robustness and improved tracking performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DFM-KD consistently outperforms conventional feature-level distillation methods in both tracking precision and success rates.

</details>


### [142] [Rotated, but How Far? Diagnosing and Improving Object-Rotation Reasoning in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05715)
*Zhaochen Wang,Yujun Cai,Huangbo Zou,Hower Yang,Naipeng Dong,Miao Xu,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OR-Bench基准和RotationCue方法，揭示视觉语言模型在物体旋转推理中存在"规范角度坍缩"问题，并通过从冻结表示中恢复旋转信息提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能检测物体在视角间发生了旋转，但无法可靠判断旋转幅度。论文希望系统评估这种能力差距，并探究其成因。

Method: 提出了OR-Bench细粒度基准，涵盖八个任务（旋转检测、旋转幅度估计、多视角旋转推理）；对12个VLM进行评估；通过表示探测分析信息可用性；提出RotationCue——一个轻量解码器，从VLM自身冻结表示中恢复粗略旋转信息，并作为中间文本上下文回馈给模型。

Result: 最强的模型在检测任务上接近100%准确率，但即使是粗略幅度估计也接近随机水平。要求精确角度时，91.8%-100%的预测集中在0°、90°、180°。表示探测显示模型已经编码了大量粗略旋转信息，简单线性探针优于模型生成的答案。RotationCue在三个VLM上提升了所有模型-任务组合，宏平均准确率提升7.9-12.6个点，且保持通用能力。

Conclusion: VLM未能充分利用其已编码的旋转信息；通过从冻结表示中恢复并回馈粗略旋转信息，可显著改善旋转推理能力，说明问题部分在于信息利用而非信息缺失。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can detect that an object has rotated across views, but cannot reliably tell by how much. We introduce OR-Bench, a fine-grained benchmark for object-rotation reasoning with eight tasks covering rotation detection, rotation magnitude estimation, and multi-view rotation reasoning. Across 12 VLMs, the gap is stark: the strongest models approach 100% accuracy on detection, yet even coarse magnitude estimation is near chance. When asked for exact angles, models place 91.8--100% of their predictions on just $0^\circ$, $90^\circ$, and $180^\circ$, a failure we term canonical-angle collapse. This collapse persists even without visual input. Representation probing shows that missing information is only part of the explanation. Although rotation information becomes less recoverable at finer granularity, substantial coarse-grained information remains, and a simple linear probe outperforms the models' generated answers. This suggests that VLMs underuse rotation information they already encode. We therefore propose RotationCue, a lightweight decoder that recovers coarse rotation information from the VLM's own frozen representations and feeds it back to the model as intermediate textual context. Across three VLMs, RotationCue improves every model--task combination on OR-Bench, raising macro-average accuracy by 7.9--12.6 points while preserving general capabilities.

</details>


### [143] [T-JEPA: A Temporal Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Learning Better Remote Sensing Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05731)
*Bowen Peng,Li Liu,Yongxiang Liu,Weijie Li,Jie Zhou,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 T-JEPA，一种时间联合嵌入预测架构，利用地球观测序列中稀疏非均匀的时间采样作为监督信号，学习以时间间隔为条件的潜在状态转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测数据具有丰富的时间监督信息，但现有遥感基础模型要么依赖预定义的成对关系，要么仅聚合整体重建上下文，未能充分利用 EO 序列中稀疏且非均匀的时间采样特性。

Method: T-JEPA 采用共享单帧编码器处理每个观测；时间预测器从被掩码的源潜在表示和实际经过时间估计完整的目标潜在场。通过多个时间间隔的预测约束将观测状态组织为结构化的潜在轨迹。采用非对称元数据注入缓解捷径学习；多时间尺度的直接监督优于递归展开中间状态。同时引入掩码像素重建提供保留空间细节的互补监督。

Result: 在匹配预训练数据和吞吐量条件下，T-JEPA 在静态与时序任务上均取得领先的迁移性能。分析表明其表示具有依赖时间间隔的转移可预测性和连贯的潜在动态，并保持较强的跨周期一致性、表示多样性与语义判别性。

Conclusion: T-JEPA 有效挖掘 EO 序列中稀疏非均匀时间采样作为监督信号，学习到时间间隔条件下的潜在转移表示，在迁移性能与表示质量上均表现优异。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) data provide rich temporal supervision, yet existing remote sensing foundation models mainly exploit sequential observations through imposing predefined pairwise relations or aggregating holistic reconstruction context. We seek to further exploit the sparse and nonuniform temporal sampling inherent in EO sequences as supervisory signals. To this end, we propose T-JEPA, a temporal joint-embedding predictive architecture that learns time-gap-conditioned latent transitions. A shared single-frame encoder processes each observation, while a temporal predictor estimates the complete target latent field from a masked source latent representation and the actual elapsed time. Across multiple temporal intervals, these predictive constraints organize observed states into structured latent trajectories. Asymmetric metadata injection mitigates shortcut learning, and direct supervision across multiple temporal scales proves more effective than recursively rolling out intermediate states. In parallel, masked pixel reconstruction provides complementary supervision for preserving spatial details. Under matched pre-training data and throughput, T-JEPA achieves leading transfer performance on both static and temporal tasks. Analyses further reveal that T-JEPA learns representations with time-gap-dependent transition predictability and coherent latent dynamics, while maintaining strong cross-period consistency, representation diversity, and semantic discriminability.

</details>


### [144] [HLA-WM: Hybrid Linear Attention for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05739)
*Zhuokun Chen,Feng Chen,Xi Lin,Xiyu Wu,Jiahao He,Jianfei Cai,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HLA-WM 是一个免训练混合线性注意力框架，通过几何引导的粗粒度检索与细粒度循环线性状态计算相结合，缓解 Gated DeltaNet 的长程遗忘问题，在长时序视频世界模型中提升场景一致性与相机控制，同时大幅降低历史状态内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序视频世界模型需要持久记忆以在长时间 rollout 中保持场景一致性。Softmax 注意力依赖不断增长的 KV cache 保留完整历史，但内存开销大；循环线性注意力以固定大小状态压缩历史，内存成本低，但存在严重的长程遗忘问题（Gated DeltaNet 中尤为明显），远处但相关的场景信息会被后续状态更新逐步削弱。

Method: 提出 HLA-WM：一种免训练的混合线性注意力框架，结合粗粒度几何引导检索与细粒度循环线性状态计算。利用 GDN 的仿射结构缓存紧凑的分块转移摘要（chunk-wise transition summaries），基于相机几何检索与场景相关的历史块，并将其重组为针对查询的循环状态。

Result: 在 60 秒 SANA-WM-Bench 上，无需额外训练即提升基线自回归生成器全部六项重访一致性与相机控制聚合指标，包括 0.74 dB PSNR 增益和 28.5% 旋转误差降低；改进在下游精修后仍保持，并泛化到 MBench-A，在 547 个样本上对全部四个子集和所有推理模式一致提升三项重访一致性指标。在 60 秒上下文下，历史状态内存相比完整 KV 缓存减少 12×，推理吞吐下降最多 1.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可选择性寻址的循环记忆既能改善长程场景召回，又能保留 GDN 的效率优势。

Abstract: Long-horizon video world models require persistent memory to preserve scene consistency over extended rollouts. Softmax attention retains the full generation history through a growing KV cache, whereas recurrent linear attention compresses history into fixed-size states with substantially lower memory cost. However, we identify severe long-range forgetting in Gated DeltaNet (GDN), where information from distant but relevant scenes is progressively attenuated by subsequent state updates. To address this limitation, we propose HLA-WM, a training-free hybrid linear-attention framework that combines coarse-grained geometry-guided retrieval with fine-grained recurrent linear-state computation. HLA-WM exploits the affine structure of GDN to cache compact chunk-wise transition summaries, retrieve scene-relevant historical chunks using camera geometry, and recompose them into query-specific recurrent states. On the $60$-second SANA-WM-Bench, HLA-WM improves all six aggregate revisit-consistency and camera-control metrics of the base autoregressive generator without additional training, including a $0.74$ dB PSNR gain and a $28.5\%$ reduction in rotation error. The improvements persist after downstream refinement and generalize to MBench-A, where HLA-WM consistently improves all three revisit-consistency metrics across all four subsets and all evaluated inference modes over $547$ samples. At a $60$-second context, HLA-WM reduces historical-state memory by $12\times$ relative to full KV caching while incurring at most a $1.6\%$ reduction in inference throughput. These results demonstrate that selectively addressable recurrent memory can improve long-range scene recall while preserving the efficiency advantages of GDN. Project page: https://caesarhhh.github.io/hla-wm/

</details>


### [145] [Robust Local Optimization Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05743)
*James Pritts,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了鲁棒损失形状、内点尺度误设与优化策略对本质矩阵、基础矩阵及单应估计中RANSAC局部优化的影响，提出剖面-边缘得分与联合半二次优化策略，并据此构建了更鲁棒的RANSAC框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RANSAC的假设评分与局部优化（LO）具有不同的鲁棒性需求，将假设选择与精化分离可以带来收益。作者希望系统厘清鲁棒损失形状、内点尺度错误设定以及优化策略选择对本质矩阵、基础矩阵和单应估计的具体影响，从而指导更可靠的RANSAC设计。

Method: 作者提出剖面-边缘得分（profile-marginal score），边缘化多余的（nuisance）内点尺度并选择一个内点划分，由此估计出设定LO损失宽度的尺度；研究不同鲁棒损失形状与优化策略（基于当前残差重加权的方法、宽盆地方法、联合半二次优化）的行为，并测试与剖面-边缘得分匹配、单调不降的优化器。

Result: 剖面-边缘得分使LO对内点尺度设定过大具有鲁棒性，但尺度设定过小会损害选择本身；精化需要来自种子当前所拒绝对应点的梯度，仅用当前残差重加权的方法会固守种子，而宽盆地方法能恢复强扰动的种子却会降低准确评分选择假设的质量，故仅凭盆地大小不足以评估RANSAC LO；联合半二次优化在各类模型间表现最一致；与剖面-边缘得分匹配且单调不降的优化器并未达到最佳精度。最终RANSAC在PhotoTourism上把SOTA方法的中位本质矩阵位姿误差从2.23度降至1.58度（尺度正确设定），在尺度严重误设（大128倍）时从38度降至6.2度。

Conclusion: 内点尺度、损失形状与优化策略需分别考量：评分与精化目标不必一致，联合半二次优化是跨模型最稳健的LO策略，所提RANSAC在本质矩阵位姿估计上显著优于现有方法，尤其在内点尺度严重误设时。

Abstract: RANSAC scoring and local optimization (LO) impose different robustness requirements, motivating the separation of hypothesis selection from refinement. We systematically isolate the effects of robust-loss shape, incorrectly specified inlier scales, and optimization strategy on essential matrix, fundamental matrix, and homography estimation. A profile-marginal score marginalizes the nuisance inlier scale and selects an inlier partition, from which we estimate the scale that sets the LO loss width; this makes LO robust to an inlier scale specified too large, whereas one specified too small degrades selection itself. Refinement needs gradient from correspondences the seed currently rejects: optimizers that reweight from current residuals stay pinned to their seed, whereas methods with broad basins recover strongly perturbed seeds yet degrade accurate score-selected hypotheses, so basin size alone is insufficient to assess RANSAC LO. Joint half-quadratic optimization balances the two and is the most consistent strategy across model classes. An optimizer matched to the profile-marginal score, which never decreases it, does not reach the best accuracy, challenging the prescription that scoring and refinement objectives should match. Composed from these findings, our RANSAC reduces the median essential-matrix pose error of a state-of-the-art RANSAC on PhotoTourism from 2.23 degrees to 1.58 degrees with a correctly specified inlier scale and from 38 degrees to 6.2 degrees when it is grossly misspecified (128x too large).

</details>


### [146] [A new design of a fall detection system integrating landmark identification and deep learning techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05749)
*Tri Nhut Do,Thi Thuy Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合关键点识别与深度学习的跌倒检测系统，通过MediaPipe空间识别区分日常动作与真实跌倒，真实场景中达到95%的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升跌倒检测的准确性与可靠性，减少误报，保障老年人安全与独立生活能力，并服务于养老照护和智能健康监测场景。

Method: 采用计算机视觉技术（如MediaPipe进行空间识别）提取关键点，并将其与深度学习预测算法相结合，以区分常规动作与真实跌倒并降低误报。

Result: 实验显示训练准确率稳步上升并在第40个周期趋于稳定，误差率在初期周期显著下降；涉及8至50岁男女参与者的实时实验获得95%的跌倒检测率，并在多种场景下表现良好。

Conclusion: 该关键点识别与深度学习融合的系统具有较强通用性和有效性，在老年照护与智能健康监测领域具有应用前景。

Abstract: This article introduces an innovative system that integrates landmark identification with deep learning to enhance fall detection accuracy and reliability. By utilizing advanced computer vision techniques, such as Media Pipe for spatial recognition, the system effectively differentiates between routine movements and actual falls. The integration of landmarks with a deep learning prediction algorithm minimizes false alarms, ensuring timely responses to genuine falls. Comprehensive experimentation underscores the system's versatility across various scenarios, emphasizing its potential to improve safety and independence for older adults. The training process demonstrates a steady increase in accuracy, stabilizing by the 40th cycle, while error rates decline significantly during the initial cycles. Real-time experiments, involving both male and female participants aged 8 to 50, recorded a remarkable 95% detection rate of falls, demonstrating the system's effectiveness and promising future applications in elder care and smart health monitoring environments.

</details>


### [147] [Vision-enabled detection of safety helmet compliance in construction zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05756)
*Tri Nhut Do*,Ba Loc Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于YOLOv8的视觉实时安全帽佩戴检测系统，用于建筑工地安全监管，检测精度超过97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑管理中工人安全是首要任务，需实时监测安全帽佩戴合规性以降低头部受伤风险。

Method: 利用战略性布置摄像头的高分辨率视频流，结合计算机视觉与机器学习算法，在YOLO框架（YOLOv8）下采用深度学习方法识别未戴安全帽人员。

Result: 训练与验证中，戴帽与未戴帽人员的mAP@0.5精度均超过97%；在不同光照条件和多样工人动作下检测精度优异，损失持续下降、指标持续提升。

Conclusion: 研究验证了YOLOv8在提升识别性能方面的有效性，其意义超越合规监管，为职业安全管理创新应用及智能建筑环境发展奠定基础。

Abstract: In the rapidly evolving field of construction management, worker safety remains a top priority. This paper introduces an innovative vision-based system for real-time detection of helmet compliance, specifically designed for construction sites, utilizing advanced computer vision techniques and machine learning algorithms within the YOLO (you only look once) framework. Our system leverages high-resolution video feeds from strategically positioned cameras to monitor adherence to safety regulations regarding helmet usage. By employing deep learning methodologies, the system effectively identifies individuals not wearing helmets, thereby significantly mitigating the risk of head injuries among workers. Our training and validation results revealed an impressive precision exceeding 97% at mAP@0.5 for both helmeted and non-helmeted individuals. Furthermore, our experiments demonstrate exceptional detection accuracy, demonstrating the system's resilience under varying lighting conditions and diverse worker movements. The consistent decrease in loss and improvement in metrics throughout training validates the effectiveness of the YOLOv8 model in enhancing recognition performance. The implications of this research extend beyond mere regulatory compliance, opening avenues for innovative applications in occupational safety management. This study highlights the critical role of technology in protecting lives and lays the groundwork for future advancements in smart construction environments.

</details>


### [148] [A Three-Dimensional Reverse-Projection Method for Sparse Point Cloud Completion and Its Application to High-Speed Train Nose Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05758)
*Xiaozhen Ma,Zhao Tang,Hanbin Lai,Ruiqi Chen,Jin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一套点云获取、滤波、补全与曲面重建的流程，解决高速列车车头玻璃窗区域扫描缺失问题。方法结合FAST-LIVO2、MLS平滑与三轴反向补全，并用三种网格重建方法对比，实验表明贪婪投影三角化几何误差最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含玻璃窗环境的LiDAR扫描存在空洞，是三维重建中尚未解决的问题；高速列车车头扫描中存在稀疏采样与玻璃窗区域点云缺失，需要一种实用的非接触式数字建模方法。

Method: 采用FAST-LIVO2通过多传感器里程计与建图获取初始点云，用移动最小二乘法（MLS）平滑观测；提出基于三轴投影的细分与插值方法，并结合反向空洞边界识别，称为三轴反向补全，从每个空洞周围的观测插值缺失区域；随后使用贪婪投影三角化、泊松曲面重建和基于Marching Cubes的流程从补全点云生成网格。

Result: 在按比例缩放的列车车头展示模型实验中，所提方法能够填补玻璃窗周围的缺失点云区域；在本文评价设置下，贪婪投影三角化的几何距离误差低于另外两种重建流程。

Conclusion: 该流程支持列车车头几何的非接触式数字建模，为重建带玻璃窗物体提供了一种实用方法。

Abstract: Holes in LiDAR scans of environments with glass windows remain an unresolved problem in three-dimensional reconstruction. This study presents a pipeline for point cloud acquisition, filtering, completion, and surface reconstruction to address sparse sampling and missing window regions in scans of a high-speed train nose. FAST-LIVO2 provides the initial point cloud through multisensor odometry and mapping, and moving least squares (MLS) smooths the observations. We then introduce three-axis projection-based subdivision and interpolation with reverse hole boundary identification, referred to as three-axis reverse completion. The method interpolates missing regions from observations around each hole. Greedy projection triangulation, Poisson surface reconstruction, and a Marching Cubes-based pipeline generate meshes from the completed point cloud. Experiments on a proportionally scaled display model of a high-speed train nose show that the proposed method fills missing point cloud regions around the glass windows. Under the evaluation setting used in this study, greedy projection triangulation yields lower geometric distance errors than the other two reconstruction pipelines. The pipeline supports non-contact digital modeling of train nose geometry and provides a practical approach to reconstructing objects with glass windows.

</details>


### [149] [Controllable Road Marking Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05771)
*Zhiyu,Cai,Yufan Zhang,Ruichen Tan,Zengxiang Lei,Satish Ukkusuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"可控道路标线生成"任务与条件BEV生成流程，从可行驶区域掩码、外环标线与文本描述合成中心区域标线布局，在Argoverse 2与Waymo上大幅超越掩码精化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路与车道标线是车辆导航和多智能体协同的重要依据，但大规模标线制作仍依赖人工流程，限制了定量分析与场景测试，因此需要可自动、可控地生成标线布局的方法。

Method: 构建使用确定性、元数据派生提示的基准，输出车道分隔线、道路分隔线和人行横道三通道。开发条件BEV流程：文本条件的隐空间rectified-flow DiT，配合拓扑感知辅助损失；采用高斯模糊训练目标以稳定稀疏细标线的学习；并提出免训练后处理SGR（Structure Gaussian Render），通过提取折线、拟合三次贝塞尔曲线并以各向异性超高斯基元重渲染恢复清晰几何。

Result: 在4,597个Argoverse 2测试瓦片上达到Buffered F1 80.8、clDice 50.2，而适配的SOTA掩码精化基线仅为38.8和24.6；在Waymo数据集上达到Buffered F1 88.0、clDice 66.2。消融显示拓扑感知监督与SGR带来互补的连通性增益；文本编辑实验表明更强的引导提升编辑成功率，但也增加对非目标结构的改动。

Conclusion: 该框架是迈向面向仿真的道路标线变化生成、自动地图补全与早期基础设施设计探索的一步。

Abstract: Lane and road markings provide critical guidance for vehicle navigation and multi-agent coordination, yet authoring them at scale remains a manual workflow that limits quantitative analysis and scenario testing. We introduce Controllable Road Marking Generation, which synthesizes a missing center-region marking layout from a drivable-area mask, optional outer-ring markings, and a textual description. Our benchmark uses deterministic, metadata-derived prompts and three output channels: lane dividers, road dividers, and pedestrian crossings. We develop a conditional bird's-eye-view (BEV) pipeline that combines (i) a text-conditioned latent rectified-flow DiT trained with a topology-aware auxiliary loss, (ii) Gaussian-blurred training targets that stabilize learning of thin, sparse markings, and (iii) Structured Gaussian Render (SGR), a training-free post-process that recovers crisp divider geometry by extracting polylines, fitting cubic Bézier curves, and re-rendering them as anisotropic super-Gaussian primitives. On 4,597 Argoverse~2 test tiles, our system achieves Buffered F1 of 80.8 and clDice of 50.2, compared with 38.8 and 24.6 for an adapted state-of-the-art mask-refinement baseline. On Waymo dataset, it yields 88.0 Buffered F1 and 66.2 clDice. Component ablations show complementary connectivity gains from topology-aware supervision and SGR. Text-editing experiments reveal that stronger guidance improves edit success but also increases changes to non-target structures. We see this framework as a step toward simulation-ready road-marking variation, automated map completion, and early-stage infrastructure design exploration.

</details>


### [150] [InteractionBench: A Real-Time Interaction Benchmark for Streaming Video Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05775)
*Enxin Song,Suhao Yu,Yifei Xu,Barbara Su,Weili Xu,Wenhao Chai,Yao Tang,Jie Deng,Haiyang Xu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出交互基准 InteractionBench，评估视频助手"该说话才说话、不该说话保持沉默"的决策能力，发现现有系统在沉默合规性上普遍失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频助手应在指令需要回应时发声、否则保持沉默，但现有评估忽视了这一"何时响应"的决策问题。离线指标无法反映在线行为，缺少衡量完整系统（模型+记忆+响应控制器）决策能力的基准。

Method: 构建 InteractionBench，覆盖查询响应、事件触发和持续更新三类任务，包含 812 个视频上的 1,060 次交互、69 个负样本流和 53 组"计数事件 vs 近似干扰"测试套件；在视频时钟上分别打分内容准确性、时间准确性和沉默合规性。

Result: 及时发声以牺牲沉默为代价：轮询式 Qwen3-VL-8B 时间准确率 77.8% 但沉默合规仅 10.9%；原生实时系统沉默合规 29.2%、时间准确率 66.8%，却在 89.9% 的负样本流上发声；无开源权重系统通过三分之一近似干扰套件；随机删除回复只是以时间换沉默；离线分数无法预测在线失败；增加克制代价高，原生系统控制器单独贡献有限，智能体系统每次轮询约 30 秒。

Conclusion: 沉默合规是当前视频助手被忽视的核心短板，及时性与克制性存在根本权衡；离线评估不足以刻画在线交互失败，需要以完整系统为单位的新基准来推动兼顾及时发声与恰当沉默的研究。

Abstract: A video assistant must speak when its instruction warrants a response and stay silent otherwise. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates this decision for the complete system of model, memory, and response controller. InteractionBench covers query responses, event triggers, and ongoing updates in 1,060 interactions over 812 videos, with 69 negative streams and 53 suites that pair counted events with look-alike near misses. It scores content accuracy, timing accuracy, and silence compliance on the video clock. Timely speech costs silence across systems. Polled Qwen3-VL-8B reaches 77.8 timing accuracy but 10.9 silence compliance. A native real-time interaction system reaches 29.2 silence compliance at 66.8 timing accuracy, yet emits on 89.9% of negative streams. No open-weight system clears a third of the near-miss suites. Fewer replies help only when chosen, as random deletion merely trades timing for silence. Offline scores miss these failures and mispredict online behavior. Adding restraint is costly, as the native system's controller adds little by itself and agentic systems add it only at about 30 s per poll.Project page: https://www.enxinsong.com/projects/interactionbench/ Code: https://github.com/Espere-1119-Song/InteractionBench Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/InteractionBench/InteractionBench

</details>


### [151] [CentriQ: Calibration-Free Quantization of Diffusion Transformers via Exact Mean Centering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06260)
*Nataša Jovanović,Mathieu Salzmann,Saqib Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiT扩散变换器采样成本高，4比特量化可降低成本，但现有方法或依赖校准或数据无关的Hadamard旋转在DiT上失效。论文指出失效的结构性原因，并提出CentriQ——一种免校准量化器，通过token中心化与秩1分支恢复均值，在多个DiT上匹配校准方法质量并首次实现2比特激活下可用的图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT虽达到SOTA图像生成，但采样成本限制部署。将权重与激活量化到4比特可降低成本，但现有方法存在问题：基于校准的方法绑定特定检查点和提示分布；对LLM有效的无数据Hadamard旋转在DiT上质量下降。

Method: 作者揭示失效的结构性原因：自适应层归一化条件化会给激活添加逐token均值，在受评估DiT宽度下，数据无关方法使用的Hadamard旋转无法将该均值均匀分散到各坐标，因此单一主方向在旋转后仍存活并决定量化范围。据此提出CentriQ，一种免校准量化器：在旋转前对每个token中心化，并通过秩1全精度分支精确恢复均值，使逐token缩放可无数据地以闭式形式得到。权重在鲁棒ℓp目标下拟合，以追踪每组的密集模式并削弱重尾影响。

Result: 在三个DiT上，CentriQ在4比特下匹配校准SVDQuant的质量，而使用普通逐token激活量化的免校准权重量化器则崩溃或显著退化。在2比特权重下，CentriQ优于迄今报道的最强免校准方法，并且是首个在2比特激活下仍能保持可用图像质量的免校准方法。

Conclusion: CentriQ通过分析Hadamard旋转在DiT上失效的结构性原因，设计了中心化加秩1均值恢复的免校准量化方案，配合鲁棒权重拟合目标，在多个DiT上实现与校准方法相当甚至更优的量化效果，拓展了免校准量化在低比特DiT生成中的可用范围。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image generation, but their sampling cost limits deployment. Quantizing both weights and activations to 4 bits reduces this cost, yet existing methods fall short in one of two ways. Calibration-based methods are tied to a specific checkpoint and prompt distribution, whereas data-free Hadamard rotation, effective for LLMs, loses quality on DiTs. We show that this loss has a structural cause. Adaptive layer-norm conditioning adds a per-token mean to the activations, and at the widths of the evaluated DiTs, the Hadamard rotations used by data-free methods cannot spread this mean uniformly across coordinates. A single dominant direction therefore survives the rotation and sets the quantization range. We introduce CentriQ, a calibration-free quantizer that centers each token before rotation and restores the mean exactly through a rank-1 full-precision branch, so that per-token scales follow in closed form without data. Weights are fitted under a robust $\ell_p$ objective that tracks the dense mode of each group and discounts heavy tails. Across three DiTs, CentriQ matches the quality of calibrated SVDQuant at 4 bits, whereas calibration-free weight quantizers with plain per-token activation quantization collapse or degrade substantially. CentriQ outperforms the strongest calibration-free method reported to date at 2-bit weights. It is also the first calibration-free method to retain usable image quality at 2-bit activations.

</details>


### [152] [A Spatiotemporal Semantic Importance-Guided Unified Compression and Editing Framework for AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05779)
*Xihua Sheng,Dong Liu,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个面向AI生成视频的统一压缩与编辑框架，以冻结的视频生成器作为可复用生成先验，设计三种时空语义重要性引导技术，分别解决传输什么、传输多少以及如何使用传输的边信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频在数量、时长和分辨率上快速增长，带来高效存储与传输需求。与自然视频不同，AI生成视频是学习到的生成分布的样本，其内容一致性依赖语义结构，而许多局部纹理和随机细节可被合理再生，因此压缩应保留重要的时空语义信息而非逐像素重建。此外，用户还需要基于提示的编辑，在修改内容的同时保持原始时空语义。

Method: 以冻结的视频生成器作为可复用生成先验，构建统一压缩与编辑框架，并设计三种时空语义重要性引导技术：一是创新选择方法，用时空语义重要性投影潜在差异，使所选创新优先关注语义不变量而非可替代的生成变化；二是帧自适应比特分配方法，估计潜在帧的非均匀语义需求，为需要更强语义保留的帧分配更多创新；三是统一重建与编辑方法，连续调整传输边信息的影响，使同一压缩表示既能强引导忠实重建，又能作为灵活语义锚点支持结构保持的提示驱动编辑。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据或量化结果，主要提出了统一压缩与编辑框架及三种语义重要性引导技术，用于支持AI生成视频的高效压缩、忠实重建和提示驱动编辑。

Conclusion: 通过利用AI生成视频的语义可再生成特性以及冻结生成先验，可在统一框架中同时实现面向语义的压缩和结构保持的提示编辑，为AI生成视频的存储、传输与交互编辑提供了一种新思路。

Abstract: AI-generated videos are rapidly increasing in volume, duration, and resolution, creating growing demands for efficient storage and transmission. Unlike natural videos captured from the physical world, AI-generated videos are samples from a learned generative distribution, where semantic structures are critical to content consistency, while many local textures and stochastic details can be plausibly regenerated. This distinction suggests that compression should preserve semantically important spatiotemporal information rather than reconstruct every pixel of a particular generative sample. Beyond reconstruction, AI-generated videos also create a practical need for prompt-based editing, where users expect to modify generated content while preserving its original spatiotemporal semantics. Motivated by these observations, we propose a unified compression and editing framework for AI-generated videos that incorporates a frozen video generator as a reusable generative prior. Within this framework, we design three spatiotemporal semantic importance-guided techniques that respectively address what to transmit, how much to transmit, and how to use the transmitted side information. First, an innovation selection method projects the latent discrepancy using spatiotemporal semantic importance, so that the selected innovations prioritize semantic invariants over replaceable generative variations. Second, a frame-adaptive bit allocation method estimates the nonuniform semantic demands of latent frames and allocates more innovations to frames requiring stronger semantic preservation. Third, a unified reconstruction and editing method continuously adjusts the influence of the transmitted side information, enabling the same compressed representation to provide strong guidance for faithful reconstruction or serve as a flexible semantic anchor for structure-preserving prompt-driven editing.

</details>


### [153] [KineWorld: Action-Induced Transport Fields for Embodied World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06349)
*Ziying Song,Yuchen Liu,Zhuoran Xu,Ziyang Liu,Jian Jin,Jiangtao Su,Haibao Yu,Lei Yang,Yuanpei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KineWorld 提出"输运感知"的世界模型框架，用机器人运动学指导生成监督的分配，在单/多视角评估中均取得较好成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型采用均匀加权的视觉生成目标，与具身预测需求不匹配；即使引入显式运动条件，仍会低估对交互至关重要的空间稀疏变化。

Method: 提出 KineWorld：(1) Kinematic Transport Lifting (KTL) 由指令运动构建渲染器导出的、相机对齐的输运场；(2) Transport-Aware World Diffusion (TAWD) 在视频潜空间网格上校准运动支撑，通过均匀分布与输运聚焦分布的归一化混合，对 future-RGB flow matching 重新加权。数据为 RoboTwin 2.0 的 ALOHA-AgileX 双臂操作数据。

Result: 单视角评估 EWMScore-P 达 68.95，多视角评估 TWB-Score 达 54.82。

Conclusion: 结果支持从外观拟合转向面向动作后果的建模，以服务具身决策。

Abstract: Embodied world models predict the visual consequences of candidate actions before execution. However, existing action-conditioned world models often adopt uniformly weighted visual generation objectives that can be misaligned with embodied prediction needs. Even with explicit motion conditioning, these objectives can underemphasize spatially sparse changes that are critical to interaction. We propose KineWorld, a transport-aware world-modeling framework that extends robot kinematics from motion conditioning to the spatial allocation of generative supervision. Kinematic Transport Lifting (KTL) constructs renderer-derived, camera-aligned transport fields from commanded robot motion. Transport-Aware World Diffusion (TAWD) calibrates their motion support on the video-latent grid and reweights future-RGB flow matching through a normalized mixture of uniform and transport-focused distributions. We train KineWorld using ALOHA-AgileX bimanual manipulation data from RoboTwin 2.0. KineWorld achieves an EWMScore-P of 68.95 in single-view evaluation and a TWB-Score of 54.82 in multi-view evaluation. These results support a shift from appearance fitting toward action-consequence modeling for embodied decision-making.

</details>


### [154] [FairRSFM: A Biome-Aware Benchmark and Debiasing Framework for Remote Sensing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05790)
*Md Aminur Hossain,Omkumar Vaghasiya,Rajeev Ranjan Dwivedi,Vinod Kurmi,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出FairRSFM，一个按生物群系（biome）划分的基准，用于评估遥感基础模型（RSFM）在不同生态区域上的鲁棒性，揭示聚合指标掩盖的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感基础模型通常用聚合指标评估，这会掩盖不同生态区域之间的系统性性能差异，导致生态群组鲁棒性问题被忽视。论文希望提供一个能诊断并缓解此类差距的评估协议。

Method: FairRSFM将14个陆地生物群系类别的地理参考样本映射为6个生态宏观组，采用统一的冻结骨干评估协议，覆盖m-EuroSAT、m-BigEarthNet、m-SA-Crop-Type和MMEarth20K（含Dynamic World标签图）四个下游数据集，并在Prithvi-EO-2.0、SatMAE、DOFA三个模型、三个随机种子上进行评测；同时评估Biome-Orthogonal Linear Probing (BOLP)、Dynamic Biome Reweighting (DBR)和GroupDRO三种缓解基线。

Result: 聚合性能一致地掩盖了依赖生物群系的差异，且跨架构与任务均有体现。例如Prithvi-EO-2.0在m-EuroSAT上总体macro-F1为90.98%，但平均最差组仅83.72%；m-SA-Crop-Type从总体mIoU 27.30%降至Xeric and Mineralogical组的18.47%。缓解方法效果依赖模型和任务，如BOLP在不更新RSFM骨干的情况下将Prithvi-EO-2.0在m-BigEarthNet上的worst-group F1@opt从46.12%提升到50.27%。

Conclusion: FairRSFM为诊断和缓解遥感基础模型中的生态鲁棒性差距提供了可复用的协议，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Remote sensing foundation models (RSFMs) are commonly evaluated using aggregate metrics, which can hide systematic performance disparities across ecological regions. We introduce FairRSFM, a biome-aware benchmark for evaluating ecological group robustness in RSFMs. FairRSFM maps georeferenced samples from 14 terrestrial biome classes into six ecologically meaningful macro-groups and evaluates models under a unified frozen-backbone evaluation protocol. The benchmark covers four downstream datasets: m-EuroSAT, m-BigEarthNet, m-SA-Crop-Type, and MMEarth20K with Dynamic World label maps. Using Prithvi-EO-2.0, SatMAE, and DOFA across three random seeds, we show that aggregate performance consistently masks biome-dependent disparities across architectures and tasks. For example, Prithvi-EO-2.0 reaches 90.98% overall macro-F1 on m-EuroSAT but a mean worst-group score of only 83.72%, while m-SA-Crop-Type drops from 27.30% overall mIoU to 18.47% in the Xeric and Mineralogical group. We further evaluate Biome-Orthogonal Linear Probing (BOLP), Dynamic Biome Reweighting (DBR), and GroupDRO as complementary mitigation baselines. Their effectiveness is model- and task-dependent; for example, BOLP improves Prithvi-EO-2.0 worst-group F1@opt on m-BigEarthNet from 46.12% to 50.27% without updating the RSFM backbone. FairRSFM provides a reusable protocol for diagnosing and mitigating ecological robustness gaps in remote sensing foundation models. Code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/aminurhossain/FairRSFM.

</details>


### [155] [RealtimeWAM: One-Step Asynchronous World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06617)
*Chengtao Lv,Jinyang Du,Shuyi Feng,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Shunzi Yang,Ruihao Gong,Shen Ren,Tianwei Zhang,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealtimeWAM 通过一步动作生成与异步推理，显著提升 World Action Models 的推理效率，近乎无损且端到端加速约 25 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效 WAM 采用 MoT 架构并复用视频表示，但仍受限于专家内多步动作去噪迭代与专家间顺序执行的等待开销。

Method: 提出教师锚定一致性蒸馏（TACD），用冻结教师多步 rollout 端点提供显式监督以弥补局部-全局误差差；并提出跨专家波前流水线（CEWP），通过分块共享视频 KV 缓存重叠两专家，仅在动作注意力消费前同步。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 及 Fast-WAM、Faster-WAM 等上验证优越性，性能近乎无损（下降&lt;1%），H100 上端到端加速约 25 倍。

Conclusion: RealtimeWAM 有效解决两步效率瓶颈，实现近无损的一步动作生成与异步推理，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) incorporate visual representations from video generation backbones to guide action prediction. Recent efficient WAMs adopt Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architectures and compute video representations once for reuse by the action expert. However, intra-expert iteration (\ie, multi-step action denoising) and inter-expert waiting (\ie, sequential execution of the video and action experts) still limit inference efficiency. To this end, we present RealtimeWAM, an extremely efficient WAM variant with one-step action generation and asynchronous inference, addressing these two bottlenecks. To reduce intra-expert iteration, we propose Teacher-Anchored Consistency Distillation (TACD) to address a local-global error gap: low local consistency error alone does not guarantee accurate final actions. TACD supplements local consistency with explicit supervision from the frozen teacher's multi-step rollout endpoint, enabling accurate one-step action generation. Additionally, we propose Cross-Expert Wavefront Pipelining (CEWP) to eliminate unnecessary expert-level waiting. It overlaps the two experts through block-wise sharing of the video KV cache, synchronizing only immediately before the corresponding action attention consumes it. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks (\eg, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin) and model variants (\eg, Fast-WAM and Faster-WAM) demonstrate the superiority of RealtimeWAM. Notably, RealtimeWAM maintains near-lossless performance (\ie, $<1\%$ drop) across these benchmarks while delivering significant end-to-end speedup (\eg, $\sim25\times$ on H100). Our code and checkpoints are available via this \href{https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V/tree/main/examples/realtimewam}{link}.

</details>


### [156] [Gauss-Map Variation for Image Denoising: Geometric Analysis and an Anderson--Accelerated Majorization--Minimization Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05801)
*Haibin Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于Gauss映射变分（GMV）的图像去噪模型，利用微分几何分析其边界保持特性，并设计Anderson加速的MM算法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像去噪中保持边缘与角点等几何结构是关键挑战，需要一种能刻画空间几何变化并具有理论保证的正则化模型。

Method: 对缩放图像图的切平面投影器空间变化进行度量，建立正则器的Gauss映射等价表示；借助管状坐标和Frenet标架等微分几何工具分析其在C²及分段C²边界上的行为；采用单位法向场与标量幅值场的双线性分解，构建Anderson加速的majorization-minimization算法，法向子问题具有显式逐点MM更新。

Result: 得到边缘与角点对比度保持的估计；对L¹和L²数据保真项证明迭代的充分下降性与有界性，并证明序列收敛到惩罚模型的临界点；在合成与自然图像上的实验表明模型具有边界保持能力，并在去除高斯与脉冲噪声方面具有竞争力。

Conclusion: GMV模型在理论与数值上均表现出良好的边界保持与去噪性能，所提优化算法具有收敛保证。

Abstract: We propose a Gauss-map variation (GMV) model for image denoising that measures the spatial variation of the tangent-plane projectors of the scaled image graph. We establish an equivalent representation of the regularizer in terms of the corresponding Gauss map and, using differential geometric tools including tubular coordinates and the Frenet frame, analyze its behavior across general $C^2$ and piecewise $C^2$ boundaries. The resulting estimates provide edge- and corner-contrast preservation properties. To solve the proposed model, we introduce a bilinear decomposition involving a unit normal field and a scalar magnitude field and develop an Anderson-accelerated majorization--minimization algorithm. The normal field subproblem admits an explicit pointwise majorization--minimization update, which is combined with an Anderson acceleration. For both $L^1$ and $L^2$ data fidelity terms, we establish sufficient decrease and boundedness of the iterates and prove that the generated sequence converges to a critical point of the penalized model. Numerical experiments on synthetic and natural images demonstrate the boundary preserving capability of the proposed model and its competitive performance in removing Gaussian and impulsive noise.

</details>


### [157] [TAPDreamer: Transferable Adversarial Patches for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06814)
*Xuanyu Lu,Fengqing Jiang,Kaiyuan Zheng,Yichen Feng,Yaorui Ding,Yuetai Li,Zhen Xiang,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TAPDreamer 攻击，仅用公开编码器构造固定局部扰动，无需查询目标策略，即可在多个世界动作模型上大幅降低任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型依赖摄像头输入，其视觉表示被操纵会影响跨任务与动作策略；现有攻击需访问目标模型输出（针对动作或预测未来进行优化），实用性受限。作者希望探索仅凭公开编码器即可迁移的本地扰动攻击。

Method: 关键洞察：patch 引起的注意力权重变化与 value 向量交互，会在 patch 覆盖范围之外广播出几乎相同的表示偏移，且跨任务观测保持稳定。基于此，TAPDreamer 使用来自单一源任务的六帧图像，最大化干净与打补丁后编码器表示之间的全局 L1 距离，构造固定局部扰动。

Result: 闭环评测中，每个基准一个冻结 patch 覆盖约 6.5% 输入：FastWAM 在 40 个 LIBERO 任务上成功率从 97.7% 降至 0.0%，在 50 个 RoboTwin 任务上从 90.8% 降至 0.0%；匹配的随机 patch 仍保留 81.5% 与 79.2% 成功率。同一 patch 使两种 DreamWAM 配置降至 2.1% 与 0.8%，使 Motus 降至 10.0%。

Conclusion: 仅保护下游动作生成不足以防此类攻击；世界动作模型的防御还必须保护共享视觉编码器免受持久局部扰动。

Abstract: World models learn to predict how their environment will evolve, making them an important foundation for general-purpose robotic control. Yet world action models depend on camera inputs whose manipulation can corrupt the visual representations used across tasks and action policies. Existing attacks on these models optimize against the victim's actions or predicted futures and therefore require access to target-model outputs. In this paper, we propose an attack, TAPDreamer, against world action models that instead uses a public encoder alone to construct a fixed local perturbation that transfers across tasks and action architectures. TAPDreamer requires no target-policy queries. Our key insight is that interactions between patch-induced changes in attention weights and value vectors broadcast a nearly identical representation shift far beyond the patch footprint, and this shift remains stable across task observations. Guided by this insight, TAPDreamer uses six frames from one source task to maximize the global L1 distance between clean and patched encoder representations. In closed-loop evaluation, one frozen patch per benchmark, covering about 6.5% of the input, reduces FastWAM's success rate from 97.7% to 0.0% across 40 LIBERO tasks and from 90.8% to 0.0% across 50 RoboTwin tasks; matched random patches retain 81.5% and 79.2% success. The same patches reduce success to 2.1% and 0.8% on two DreamWAM configurations and to 10.0% on Motus. These results show that protecting downstream action generation alone is insufficient: defenses for world action models must also secure shared visual encoders against persistent local perturbations.

</details>


### [158] [Level-of-Token Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05816)
*Kiyohiro Nakayama,Brian Chao,Jan Ackermann,Hansheng Chen,Federico Tombari,Leonidas Guibas,Lior Yariv,Gordon Wetzstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Level-of-Token (LoT) Diffusion，用可变大小矩形token构成多分辨率布局，根据预知细节分布自适应分配计算，在保持预训练生成先验的同时实现图像与视频生成的质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像与视频扩散模型对每个区域分配相同计算量，但场景各处细节需求不同；且细节的空间分布往往可在生成前预知，说明部分区域计算可减少。

Method: 将细节先验转为显式的多分辨率token布局：token为不同大小和形状的矩形patch，细节处用细token、其他处用粗token；通过patch-wise非对称flow参数化与多分辨率token嵌入适配预训练扩散transformer，在每步去噪中保持全分辨率flow预测，但只处理缩减后的token序列。

Result: 用语义掩码、边界框、纹理方差、景深线索及agentic计划等生成布局进行验证；在图像和视频生成上取得有利的质量-效率权衡，加速幅度由布局token预算决定。

Conclusion: LoT Diffusion可在保持预训练生成先验的同时实现布局自适应生成，为扩散模型提供可预测、可控制的高效计算分配方案。

Abstract: Image and video diffusion models allocate equal computation to every region, even when the intended scene calls for varying levels of detail. The spatial distribution of detail can often be anticipated before generation, indicating where computation can be reduced. We introduce Level-of-Token (LoT) Diffusion, a framework that turns this knowledge into an explicit multiresolution token layout (Level-of-Token layout) for adaptive and efficient generation. Tokens represent rectangular patches of varying sizes and shapes, allocating finer tokens where detail is needed and coarser tokens elsewhere. We adapt pretrained diffusion transformers to LoT layouts through a patch-wise asymmetric flow parametrization and embeddings for multiresolution tokens, preserving full-resolution flow prediction at every denoising step while processing only a reduced token sequence. LoT Diffusion enables layout-adaptive generation while preserving pretrained generative priors. We demonstrate LoT with layouts derived from semantic masks, bounding boxes, texture variance, and depth-of-field cues, as well as agentic plans. Across image and video generation, LoT offers favorable quality-efficiency tradeoffs, with significant speedups determined by the layout's token budget. Our project website is at https://georgenakayama.github.io/lotdiffusion/.

</details>


### [159] [Imagine to Act: High-Fidelity Data Synthesis via Image Editing World Model for Scalable GUI Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05861)
*Yongxin Ning,Runliang Niu,Qianli Xing,Zhiyi Duan,Qingzu He,Pan Wang,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Infinite-Dreamer，一种基于像素级图像编辑世界模型的无仿真数据合成方法，用于生成 GUI 智能体训练所需的视觉-动作轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体的训练依赖大量高保真视觉-动作轨迹，但这类数据获取困难。人工演示难以扩展，而现有 GUI 世界模型依赖文本描述或 HTML 渲染，丢失了图标、布局样式等关键像素级视觉细节。

Method: 将 GUI 状态转移建模为图像编辑任务，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）把动作引起的 UI 变化描述为结构化的 delta-text，再微调图像编辑骨干网络，从而可控地合成逼真的截图过渡。基于该模型生成单帧视觉鲁棒性数据与多步想象轨迹。

Result: 仅在合成数据上微调 Qwen3-VL 得到 Infinite-Actor，在 AndroidWorld、MobileWorld、AndroidControl-Curated 三个基准上均优于基线：Infinite-Actor-8B 在 AndroidWorld 上 Pass@1 提升 +4.45，MobileWorld 的 Pass@3 成功率接近翻倍；Infinite-Actor-2B 的 Pass@1 提升 +9.05。

Conclusion: 像素级图像编辑世界模型能够有效合成高保真 GUI 轨迹数据，显著提升 GUI 智能体的性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for automating complex digital workflows across diverse applications. However, training highly capable and generalizable agents fundamentally relies on massive, high-fidelity visual-action trajectories, which are notoriously difficult to acquire. While human demonstrations are unscalable, existing GUI world models rely on text descriptions or HTML rendering, discarding crucial pixel-level visual details like icons and layout styles. To address this issue, we introduce Infinite-Dreamer, a simulation-free data synthesis method powered by a pixel-level Image Editing World Model. By conceptualizing GUI transitions as image editing tasks, we leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to describe action-induced UI changes as structured delta-text. We then fine-tune an image editing backbone to controllably synthesize realistic screenshot transitions. We utilize this model to generate both single-frame visual robustness data and multi-step imaginary trajectories. To validate the effectiveness of our approach, we fine-tune the Qwen3-VL baseline solely on the synthesized data to obtain Infinite-Actor, and evaluate it on AndroidWorld, MobileWorld, and AndroidControl-Curated benchmarks. Infinite-Actor consistently outperforms the Qwen3-VL baselines across scales: Infinite-Actor-8B improves AndroidWorld Pass@1 by +4.45 and nearly doubles the MobileWorld Pass@3 success rate, while Infinite-Actor-2B improves Pass@1 by +9.05. Code is available at https://github.com/swaydy-n/Infinite-Dreamer.

</details>


### [160] [Weave Mamba Fusion: Global Cross-Scale Interaction for Lightweight Face Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05865)
*Dohun Kim,Jinmyung Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Weave Mamba Fusion（WMF）与WeaveBiFPN，通过交错两相邻金字塔尺度实现跨尺度交互，构建WeaveFace检测器，在WIDER FACE上以0.34M参数达到91.41% mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征金字塔方法（FPN、BiFPN）依赖局部融合，难以建模无约束条件下（小尺度、遮挡、极端姿态）人脸检测所需的全局跨尺度依赖；状态空间模型（如Mamba）虽能提供线性复杂度的全局上下文，但其扫描需将两尺度合并为单一特征图，求和会破坏跨尺度结构，拼接则成本过高。

Method: 提出Weave Mamba Fusion（WMF）：将两个相邻金字塔尺度按列交错排列，使水平双向SS2D扫描每一步在两个尺度间切换；结合部分通道处理与无参数de-weaving，在保持特征结构的同时实现高效跨尺度交互。将WMF集成到每个融合节点得到WeaveBiFPN，作为WeaveFace检测器的neck。

Result: 在WIDER FACE上，WeaveFace以仅0.34M参数和1.16 GFLOPs达到91.41%平均AP，优于0.5M参数以下的先前检测器；在Hard子集上提升最大，达到87.14% AP。

Conclusion: WMF通过交错扫描有效保留了跨尺度结构，实现了高效的全局跨尺度融合，验证了状态空间模型在轻量级人脸检测中的潜力，尤其对困难样本效果显著。

Abstract: Feature pyramid methods, from FPN to BiFPN, have achieved strong performance in face detection by fusing multi-scale features. However, detecting faces under unconstrained conditions, such as small scale, occlusion, and extreme pose, remains difficult, as it requires global cross-scale dependencies that local fusion cannot model. State space models such as Mamba provide global context with linear complexity by scanning features as a sequence, and therefore offer a promising direction for this problem. Nevertheless, such a scan needs the two pyramid scales combined into a single feature map, and the way they are combined determines whether cross-scale structure is preserved. Summation collapses the two scales before the scan, so the scan has no cross-scale structure to exploit, while concatenation keeps both scales but at far higher cost. To address this, we propose \textbf{Weave Mamba Fusion (WMF)}, which interleaves two adjacent pyramid scales column by column so that each step of a horizontal bidirectional SS2D scan moves from one scale to the other. With partial-channel processing and parameter-free de-weaving, WMF enables efficient cross-scale interaction while preserving feature structure. Integrating WMF into every fusion node yields \textbf{WeaveBiFPN}, the neck of our \textbf{WeaveFace} detector. On WIDER FACE, WeaveFace achieves 91.41\% mean AP with only 0.34M parameters and 1.16 GFLOPs, outperforming prior detectors under 0.5M parameters. Its largest gains are on the Hard subset, where it reaches 87.14\% AP. The code is publicly available at \url{https://github.com/dohun-mat/WeaveMambaFusion}.

</details>


### [161] [Certification of Real Images through Calibrated Content Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05870)
*Sarim Hashmi,Abdelrahman Elsayed,Mohammed Talha Alam,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出深度伪造检测器准确性随时间下降（99.5%→76%），对抗扰动可令所有基线降至2%以下；作者认为内容本身无法判定真实来源，转而提出"可信重建+校准"的检测范式，输出"真实性是否可合理否认"的校准预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型能合成高质量伪造内容并被大规模滥用。评估发现20个检测器面对近四年10个生成器时准确率持续下降，且对抗扰动可轻易翻转检测结果。作者认为根本原因在于生成器可完全复现真实内容（如记忆化），因此仅凭内容无法揭示真实来源标签，需要新的检测思路。

Method: 提出一种检测范式：若内容能被某个已知生成器忠实重建，则其"合成来源"是合理的、"真实性"是可合理否认的；反之则不然。检测器输出经过校准的预测，判断真实性是否可合理否认，并用校准约束"已知生成器内容无法被复现"的发生频率。为应对自适应攻击，还在被攻击样本上校准更严格的安全阈值。

Result: (i) 检测器可校准至最多1%的生成内容被错误认证，而大多数基线在此工作点召回率接近零，最强的基线虽有93%准确率也同样失效；(ii) 在更严格的安全阈值下，该界限在有界扰动攻击空间内对自适应攻击者依然成立（此类扰动可击破所有基线），但不能覆盖任意对抗变换；(iii) 事后可验证性正在退化：3000张Reddit图片中有1116张无法被2022年生成器复现，而2024年生成器仅有55至79张无法复现。

Conclusion: 内容层面的真伪判定本质上不可靠，检测应转向"可合理否认性"的校准预测。该范式在受限攻击空间内稳健，但随着生成器能力提升，事后可验证性正迅速被侵蚀，该方法的有效性窗口可能收窄。

Abstract: Generative models can synthesize high-quality inauthentic multimedia content that is already being misused at scale. We evaluate twenty deepfake detectors against ten generators released in the last four years and find accuracy decreasing over time, from near-perfect 99.5% to 76%. Adversarial perturbations further reduce every baseline detector to below 2% accuracy, effectively inverting the detector's assigned label. We argue that this unreliability reflects a fundamental ambiguity: generators can reproduce authentic content exactly (e.g., through memorization), so content alone cannot reveal the true provenance label.For this reason, content produced by a generator must admit a faithful reconstruction by that same generator, and finding such a reconstruction makes synthetic provenance plausible and authenticity plausibly deniable.We therefore propose and evaluate a detection paradigm that outputs a calibrated prediction of whether authenticity is plausibly deniable: a faithful reconstruction by any known generator establishes plausible deniability, while calibration bounds how often content from known generators fails to be reproduced. Our evaluation shows that (i) our detector can be calibrated so that at most 1% of generated content is wrongly certified, an operating point at which most baseline detectors reach near-zero recall, including the strongest with 93% accuracy; (ii) calibrating a stricter security threshold on attacked samples preserves this bound against adaptive adversaries within the evaluated bounded-perturbation attack space, whose perturbations break every baseline, but does not cover arbitrary adversarial transformations; and (iii) post-hoc verifiability is eroding, as 1,116 of 3,000 Reddit images resist reproduction by a 2022 generator, but only 55 to 79 resist reproduction by 2024 generators.

</details>


### [162] [fMRI-TAMCL: Text-Anchored Supervised Multimodal Contrastive Learning for fMRI-Based Brain Disorder Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05880)
*Juliana Mantebea Danso,Enoch Opanin Gyamfi,Mylene C. Q. Farias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 fMRI-TAMCL 框架，通过生成受试者自适应文本，将 fMRI 图像、稀疏功能连接与文本三模态对齐融合，提升静息态 fMRI 脑疾病分类的跨站点泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静息态 fMRI 数据高度多模态且存在强烈的跨站点异质性；现有方法融合图像、BOLD 功能连接与表型数据，但 rs-fMRI 数据集通常缺乏文本模态，需依赖固定假设从表型或 BOLD 激活生成文本，导致跨数据集泛化能力差。

Method: fMRI-TAMCL 为文本锚定的多模态对比学习框架，整合 fMRI 图像、稀疏 FC 和生成的受试者特定文本。其中受试者自适应阈值推导模块生成 BOLD 激活文本，特征值序列化模块生成表型文本；三模态均编码为聚类图，投影至共享单位超球面空间，通过成对、文本锚定的监督对比学习进行对齐，并利用注意力机制融合。

Result: 在五个数据集上验证泛化能力，优于 29 个基线方法，下游分类准确率达 78.6%–86.4%。

Conclusion: fMRI-TAMCL 通过受试者自适应文本生成与文本锚定的多模态对比学习，有效缓解跨站点异质性问题，在多个数据集上展现出优于现有方法的泛化性能。

Abstract: Resting-state fMRI is important in the classification of brain disorders, but highly multimodal and exhibits strong multisite heterogeneity. Existing methods fuse images, BOLD-based functional connectivity, and phenotypic data modalities. Unlike other medical imaging datasets, rs-fMRI datasets rarely include a text modality, so they are generated from phenotypic data or BOLD activations. These text generation methods rely on fixed assumptions for subjects, sites, devices, and protocols, leading to poor generalization across datasets. We propose fMRI-TAMCL, a text-anchored multimodal contrastive learning framework that integrates fMRI images, sparse FC, and generated subject-specific text. Its Subject-Adaptive Threshold Derivation module generates BOLD activation text, while Feature-Value Serialization module generates phenotypic text. All three modalities are encoded as clustered graphs, projected onto a shared unit hypersphere space, aligned using pairwise, text-anchored supervised contrastive learning, and fused with attention. fMRI-TAMCL proves its generalization capability across five datasets outperforming 29 baselines with 78.6%-86.4% accuracy in downstream classification.

</details>


### [163] [Spatial Supervision Without Attribution Optimization: Improving Post-Hoc Class Activation Maps via Box-Guided Evidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05891)
*Wenhao Liang,Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Lin Yue,Wei Emma Zhang,Mingyu Guo,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BGER（Box-Guided Evidence Routing），通过廉价的框/掩码空间监督训练一个轻量门控来路由分类证据，从而在不对归因图做任何优化的情况下提升分类器自身 Grad-CAM 的空间合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规训练并不鼓励 CAM 在空间上合理，但 CAM 又是检验分类器预测依据的标准工具；作者希望探究能否用廉价的空间监督来改善分类器自身预测类 Grad-CAM 的质量，而无需直接优化归因图。

Method: BGER 在最终特征图上训练一个轻量门控（使用框或掩码监督），分类经由门控特征进行；而 Grad-CAM 在门控前的表示上单独计算，因此被评估的归因图从未进入训练目标。采用 BCE 路由损失，并设置匹配的对照实验（如分类绕过门控、门控梯度截断）来解耦机制来源。

Result: 在精度相当的情况下，BGER 将 MaxBoxAccV2 在 CUB-200-2011 上从 0.584 提升到 0.715，在 Stanford Dogs 上从 0.757 提升到 0.832。对照实验表明 ResNet-50 的提升主要来自空间监督重塑骨干网络而非路由本身：绕过门控时大部分提升仍存在，截断门控梯度后 ResNet-50 结果几乎不变。同样的梯度截断在两种 DenseNet-121 胸片设置中保留大部分增益，但在 Swin-T 上增益消失；直接监督 CAM 可获得更强定位，但精度代价更大。

Conclusion: 空间监督确实能改善单独评估的后验 CAM，但其作用机制和收益大小依赖于网络架构与评估设置。

Abstract: Post-hoc class activation maps (CAMs) are a standard tool for inspecting the evidence behind an image classifier's predictions, yet nothing in ordinary training encourages these maps to be spatially appropriate. We study whether inexpensive spatial supervision can improve a classifier's own predicted-class Grad-CAM without ever optimizing an attribution map. Box-Guided Evidence Routing (BGER) trains a lightweight gate on the final feature map under box or mask supervision and routes classification through the gated features, while Grad-CAM is computed separately at the pre-gate representation, so the evaluated map never enters the training objective. With a BCE routing loss, BGER raises MaxBoxAccV2 from $0.584$ to $0.715$ on CUB-200-2011 and from $0.757$ to $0.832$ on Stanford Dogs at comparable accuracy. Matched controls attribute most of the ResNet-50 gain to the spatial supervision reshaping the backbone rather than to routing itself: when classification bypasses the gate, most of the improvement remains, and detaching gradients through the gate leaves the ResNet-50 result nearly unchanged. The same detachment preserves most of the gain in two DenseNet-121 chest X-ray settings but removes the apparent gain on Swin-T, and directly supervising the CAM reaches stronger localization at a larger accuracy cost. Overall, spatial supervision can improve separately evaluated post-hoc CAMs, but both the mechanism and the size of the benefit depend on the architecture and the evaluation setting.

</details>


### [164] [TasteRoute: Personalized Routing for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05896)
*Zhi Rui Tam,Chao-Chung Wu,Sin-Han Yang,Peyton Ku,Brendan Kuang,Tzu-Ting Hsieh,Min-Fang Hsu,Fang-Ling Tsai,Yun-Nung Chen,Wei-Chiu Ma,Chieh-Yen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TasteRoute，一种个性化视频生成路由器，根据请求、用户偏好与生成预算联合选择生成模型，并发布TasteRoute-3k数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型在能力与生成成本上差异很大，如何为每个请求高效选择合适的模型成为一个自然问题。而标注者之间的偏好一致性很低（即使以他人共识作为oracle，也仅有34-55%与个体最爱一致），说明不存在单一"最佳"模型，需要个性化路由。

Method: 提出TasteRoute，基于输入请求、用户偏好信号以及可用的生成预算，联合地进行个性化视频生成模型选择（路由）。在文本到视频与图像到视频两类设置下进行评估，并与强简单基线在偏好路由上对比，同时考察平均生成成本。

Result: 在文本到视频和图像到视频场景中，TasteRoute在偏好路由方面与强简单基线具有竞争力，同时降低了平均生成成本；在更高预算上限下成本节省更为明显。

Conclusion: 个性化、成本感知的视频生成路由是可行且有益的，并发布了TasteRoute-3k多模型视频比较、质量判断、偏好排序和用户画像信号的人工标注数据集以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Rapid progress in video generation has led to a plethora of models that differ substantially in capability and generation cost. This raises a natural question: can each request be efficiently routed to an appropriate model? We find that even when the consensus of the other annotators is used as an oracle, it agrees with each annotator's own favorite only 34-55% of the time. Motivated by this observation, we introduce TasteRoute, a personalized video-generation router that selects a generator jointly based on the input request, user preferences, and available generation budget. Across text-to-video and image-to-video settings, TasteRoute is competitive with strong simple baselines on preference routing while reducing average generation cost. The cost saving increases under higher budget caps. Finally, we release TasteRoute-3k, a human-annotated dataset containing multi-model video comparisons, quality judgments, preference rankings, and user-profile signals to facilitate future research on personalized and cost-aware video routing.

</details>


### [165] [Fitting Vision Adapters at Frontier Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05897)
*Jaehoon Lee,Harry Partridge,Mudith Jayasekara,Charles O'Neill,Max Kirkby,Michael Psenka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 训练一个50M参数的小型投影器，将冻结的视觉编码器接入纯语言模型（GLM 5.2/5.3），研究语言模型规模对多模态能力的影响，并提供可复现的大规模适配器训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在冻结视觉编码器与语言模型之间训练小型投影器是多模态学习的成熟方法。但随语言模型参数量剧增，需要重新审视：在保持预训练权重不变的情况下，这一方法能赋予模型哪些视觉能力。

Method: 从 Kimi K2.6 的视觉编码器训练一个50M参数的投影器，接入本身不具备原生视觉能力的 GLM 5.2 和 5.3，并提出可复现的大规模适配器训练方案。研究两个问题：(a) 当仅语言模型侧扩展时多模态视觉能力如何缩放；(b) 哪些具体视觉能力可在规模上注入纯语言模型，哪些仍受限。

Result: 在 MMMU-Pro 和 BLINK 上评估，考察整体性能以及各单项视觉任务的表现。

Conclusion: （摘要未给出明确结论，主要贡献为可复现训练方案及对视觉能力缩放与可注入能力边界的实证研究。）

Abstract: Training a small projector between a frozen vision encoder and language model is an established approach to multimodal learning. As the parameter count of language models scales dramatically, we revisit which vision capabilities this approach can add while keeping their pretrained weights fixed. Here we train a 50M parameter projector from the vision encoder of Kimi K2.6 to GLM 5.2 and 5.3, both models without native vision capabilities, and further present a reproducible recipe for training these adapters at scale. We study the following: (a) how vision capabilities of multimodal models scale as purely the language model side scales, and (b) what specific vision capabilities are able to be imbued into a pure language model at scale, and which ones remain limited. We evaluate on MMMU-Pro and BLINK, examining both overall performance and results on individual visual tasks.

</details>


### [166] [LoDEOT: Low-Dimensional and Efficient Offset Tokens for Building Footprint Extraction from Off-Nadir Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05899)
*Kai Li,Zigan Zhou,Zhenyang Li,Hui Shan,Zhe Chen,Yupeng Deng,Zhihao Xi,Yu Meng,Yifan Peng,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出LoDEOT，用五维偏移token替代高维实例token进行屋顶到足迹偏移(RFO)预测，通过结构先验设计紧凑token，在五个真实建筑数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Instance-level RFO prediction是离轴影像提取建筑足迹的核心问题。现有query-based流程通常用高维实例token来预测二维有符号RFO，本文探索是否能改用紧凑的偏移token。

Method: 在局部针孔投影和垂直拉伸假设下，理想化RFO映射存在五参数充分描述符（本征形状、复合幅度、相对几何），为五维偏移token提供结构先验。LoDEOT保留高维实例token用于检测和分割，同时将实例token、浓度门控屋顶与box-mask证据映射为五维偏移token，再经独立的二维读数。利用已知去噪query目标索引，使各监督解码层估计对齐同一干净实例RFO。

Result: 在五个真实建筑数据集上验证。在BONAI上，LoDEOT取得最佳屋顶检测bAP和bAP50，并在所评估的端到端方法中领先全部五项偏移校正足迹指标，FAP50为54.58、mEPE为5.23像素；FAP50比端到端基线高出7.56-16.85个百分点。

Conclusion: 五维偏移token足以支持准确的RFO预测，LoDEOT对建筑足迹提取有效。

Abstract: Instance-level roof-to-footprint offset (RFO) prediction is central to extracting building footprints from off-nadir imagery. Query-based pipelines commonly use high-dimensional instance tokens to predict signed two-dimensional RFOs. We investigate whether RFO prediction can instead use a compact offset token. Under local pinhole projection and vertical-extrusion assumptions, the idealized RFO map admits a five-parameter sufficient descriptor comprising intrinsic shape, composite amplitude, and relative geometry. This factorization provides a structural prior for a five-dimensional offset token, whose channels learn task-relevant latent representations through end-to-end training. Based on this design, we propose LoDEOT, which retains high-dimensional instance tokens for detection and segmentation but maps instance-token, concentration-gated roof, and box-mask evidence to a five-dimensional offset token followed by an independent two-dimensional readout. Known denoising-query target indices further align each supervised decoder-layer estimate with the same clean instance RFO, organizing successive predictions as target-aligned recovery under perturbed query conditions. Experiments on five real-world building datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of LoDEOT for building footprint extraction. Experiments on real-world building datasets demonstrate that a five-dimensional offset token can support accurate RFO prediction. On BONAI, LoDEOT achieves the best roof-detection bAP and bAP50 and leads all five offset-corrected footprint metrics among the evaluated end-to-end methods, with FAP50 of 54.58 and mEPE of 5.23 pixels. Its FAP50 exceeds those of the evaluated end-to-end baselines by 7.56-16.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [167] [End-to-End Autonomous Recursive Arborescence Deformable Flow and Non-Linear Hemodynamics for Patient-Specific Coronary Centerline Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05900)
*Zeyu Jia,Xin Ming*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端框架，将连续几何树生成与非线性血流动力学解耦，实现患者特异性血管树提取及缺血评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统体素分割易切断分叉，启发式欧氏最小生成树引入非解剖捷径；线性泊肃叶流忽略狭窄处二次动能耗散，低估缺血。

Method: 1) 自主3D开口地标定位头（双窦查询通道+球门精修）；2) 空间接地的可变形步进流架构，三线性采样连续3D特征金字塔并施加锚点边界X(0)=P_start；3) 自顶向下递归树状态机，用Tree-NMS检测分叉峰并以父指针保证单连通无环树拓扑；4) 迭代Picard非线性Kirchhoff求解器结合Young-Tsai/Gould二次耗散。

Result: 开口定位队列平均误差7.63 mm（LCA 7.43，RCA 7.83；71.4%≤8.0 mm）。标准化狭窄测试中，线性泊肃叶流在14/14例将75%直径病变误判为非缺血（FFR>0.80）；非线性求解器捕捉功能性缺血（FFR=0.5864，病变压差32.89 mmHg，p=6.10e-5），侧支分流3.66倍。质量守恒残差5.82e-11 mL/s。测试集防火墙隔离保持。

Conclusion: 该解耦框架可从原始造影中稳健提取患者特异性血管树，并准确评估狭窄功能性缺血。

Abstract: Extracting patient-specific vascular trees from volumetric medical images is fundamental to computational angiography and non-invasive hemodynamic assessment. Conventional voxel segmentation models often sever delicate bifurcations, while heuristic Euclidean Minimum Spanning Trees introduce non-anatomical shortcuts. Moreover, linear Poiseuille flow neglects quadratic kinetic dissipation across arterial narrowings, underestimating ischemia. We formulate an end-to-end framework decoupling continuous geometric arborescence generation from non-linear hemodynamics. First, an autonomous 3D Ostium Landmark Localization Head with dual-sinus query channels and spherical-gated refinement eliminates centerline seeding dependency, achieving cohort mean localization error of 7.63 mm (7.43 mm LCA, 7.83 mm RCA; 71.4% <= 8.0 mm) from raw contrast context. Second, a Spatially-Grounded Deformable Step Flow Architecture queries continuous 3D feature pyramids via trilinear sampling, sequentially generating trajectories with anchor boundary enforcement (X(0) = P_start). Third, a Top-Down Recursive Arborescence State Machine detects bifurcation peaks via Tree-NMS and parameterizes predecessor parent pointers (p_k < k), guaranteeing single connected acyclic tree topology (beta_0 = 1, beta_1 = 0) with differentiable step termination. Fourth, an iterative Picard non-linear Kirchhoff solver with Young-Tsai / Gould quadratic dissipation enforces machine-precision mass conservation (residual 5.82e-11 mL/s). Across 14 development patients under standardized in-silico stenosis stress testing (Q_0 = 4.0 mL/s), linear Poiseuille flow misclassifies 75% diameter lesions as non-ischemic (FFR > 0.80) in 14/14 cases, whereas our non-linear solver captures functional ischemia (FFR = 0.5864, lesion disparity 32.89 mmHg, p = 6.10e-5) with 3.66x collateral shunting. Test set firewall isolation was maintained.

</details>


### [168] [Safe Image Generation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05908)
*Eungyeol Han,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生成过程中的安全框架，通过监测去噪轨迹检测NSFW信号，并用强化学习引导生成安全图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型仍会生成NSFW内容，而现有安全机制仅限于生成前过滤或生成后审核，无法应对对抗攻击在生成过程中的广泛操作空间，缺乏生成过程内的干预机制。

Method: 提出生成内（in-generation）安全框架：监测去噪轨迹，从中间表示中检测涌现的NSFW信号；并采用强化学习，从NSFW提示生成安全图像，将生成内检测与可控引导相结合。

Result: 在标准与对抗评估集上，方法持续优于现有安全图像生成方法，同时保持感知质量和提示保真度。

Conclusion: 将生成内检测与可控引导相结合，可缓解生成已开始后才出现的NSFW信号所导致的轨迹，实现更安全且保真的图像生成。

Abstract: Recent Text-to-Image (T2I) models achieve remarkable visual image generation performance, but they can still generate NSFW (Not-Safe-For-Work) contents, including violent or explicit images. Existing safety checker mechanisms are largely confined to pre-generation filtering (e.g. prompt-level text classifiers) or post-hoc moderation applied after an image is completely synthesized. However, adversarial attack methods operate over a much broader space. This imbalance highlights the need for a safety mechanism that intervenes during the generation process. We propose an in-generation safety framework that monitors the denoising trajectory and detects emerging NSFW signals from intermediate representations. Rather than merely detecting NSFW generations, our method applies reinforcement learning to generate safe images from NSFW prompts. By coupling in-generation detection with controllable steering, our approach mitigates unsafe trajectories even when NSFW signals emerge after generation has already begun. Experiments results show that our method consistently outperforms existing safe image generation methods across both standard and adversarial evaluation sets, while preserving perceptual quality and prompt fidelity. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [169] [AstraSR: Real-World Thermal Super-Resolution with GPT-6 Astra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05910)
*Mengyuan Li,Changhong Fu,Jun Zhang,Ziyu Lu,Yuhang Zhang,Haobo Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AstraSR，一种由GPT-6 Astra引导的真实世界热成像超分辨率方法，通过构建捕获LR热图像与GPT生成HR参考的图像对，并采用直接生成式监督策略，在热清晰度和结构连贯性上超越现有七种最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界热成像超分辨率受限于传感器分辨率和难以获取对应的高分辨率观测用于直接监督。传统方法将带真实退化的捕获热图像当作HR参考，再施加预定义退化生成合成LR输入，这既引入合成LR与捕获LR之间的域差距，又把采集退化保留在监督信号中。

Method: 提出AstraSR，由前沿多模态生成模型GPT-6 Astra引导。用捕获的LR热图像作为条件生成GPT-based HR参考，构建图像对数据集；开发直接生成式监督策略，从捕获热输入与GPT生成HR参考的配对中学习，并用像素、梯度和感知损失联合监督强度模式、结构边界和视觉细节的迁移。

Result: 与七种现有最先进真实世界SR方法的定性比较显示，热场景中具有连续的目标轮廓、清晰的结构边界和平滑的强度过渡，在热清晰度和结构连贯性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: AstraSR通过GPT引导的HR参考和直接生成式监督，有效缓解了域差距与退化监督问题，在真实世界热成像超分辨率任务上实现了更优的热清晰度和结构连贯性。

Abstract: Real-world thermal super-resolution (SR) is constrained by limited sensor resolution and the difficulty of obtaining corresponding high-resolution (HR) observations for direct model supervision. Conventional SR methods typically construct training pairs by treating captured thermal images with real-world degradations as HR references and applying predefined degradation to generate synthetic low-resolution (LR) inputs. Such a construction not only introduces a domain gap between synthetic and captured LR observations but also retains acquisition degradations in the supervision. To address this issue, we propose AstraSR, a real-world thermal SR method guided by GPT-6 Astra, a frontier multimodal generative model endowed with emergent and transformative visual capabilities. Specifically, we construct a dataset of image pairs by using captured LR thermal images to condition GPT-based HR reference. We develop a direct generative supervision strategy that learns from captured thermal inputs paired with GPT-generated HR references. Pixel, gradient, and perceptual losses jointly supervise the transfer of intensity patterns, structural boundaries, and visual details from the generated references. Qualitative comparisons with seven existing state-of-the-art real-world SR methods show continuous object contours, distinct structural boundaries, and smooth intensity transitions in the thermal scenes. These results demonstrate that AstraSR outperforms existing real-world SR methods in both thermal clarity and structural coherence.

</details>


### [170] [Every View Counts: View-Consistent Panoptic Quality for Multi-view Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05911)
*Youngmin Lee,Byungha Ko,Guhnoo Yun,Dong Hwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VC-PQ，一种将PQ从单图扩展到多输入视图的全景分割评估指标，强调视图一致性并将其分解为掩码精度、视图一致性和匹配阈值三部分，与PQ^scene并行报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图/前馈3D模型的预测多用从逐场景优化方法借来的PQ^scene评估，通常在渲染的留出视图上计算。PQ^scene将场景所有视图拼成单张图，导致某实例的漏检或ID变化只按其面积比例降低匹配对得分，无法反映视图一致性。

Method: 提出View-Consistent Panoptic Quality（VC-PQ）：将PQ从单图扩展到一组输入视图，对实例可见的每个视图等权计数，并惩罚预测与真值不在相同视图可见的情况；对VC-PQ进行分解，归因得分损失到掩码精度、视图一致性和匹配阈值；增加单个参数可恢复拼接的面积加权以作对比。

Result: 在ScanNet++和ScanNetv2上以固定评估协议，用VC-PQ与PQ^scene评估近期前馈方法，分解显示各方法的失分来源；对真值进行受控扰动表明VC-PQ对实例漏检或ID变化的视图数量敏感，而PQ^scene对其面积敏感。

Conclusion: 目的是让视图一致性成为多视图全景分割评估的一部分，建议在报告PQ^scene的同时报告VC-PQ。

Abstract: Multi-view panoptic segmentation assigns a semantic class and a scene-level instance ID to every pixel of an unordered set of images, and recent feed-forward 3D models predict these labels for the input views in a single forward pass. Their predictions, however, have been evaluated with the scene-level PQ (PQ^scene) borrowed from per-scene optimization methods, typically on rendered held-out views. PQ^scene tiles all views of a scene into a single image, so that a missed appearance or a change of ID lowers the score of the matched pair only in proportion to its area. We propose View-Consistent Panoptic Quality (VC-PQ), which extends PQ from a single image to a set of input views, counts equally every view in which an instance is visible, and penalizes a prediction that is not visible in the same views as its ground truth. A decomposition of VC-PQ attributes the score a method loses to mask accuracy, view consistency, and the matching threshold. A single additional parameter recovers the area weighting of tiling for comparison. Under a fixed evaluation protocol on ScanNet++ and ScanNetv2, recent feed-forward methods are evaluated with VC-PQ and PQ^scene, and the decomposition shows where each of them loses its score. Controlled perturbations of the ground truth show that VC-PQ responds to the number of views in which an instance is missed or changes ID, whereas PQ^scene responds to their area. The aim of this work is to make view consistency part of the evaluation of multi-view panoptic segmentation, with VC-PQ reported alongside PQ^scene.

</details>


### [171] [Prompt and Refinement: Asymmetric Mutual Learning for Infrared Small Target Detection with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05918)
*Yimin Fu,Songbo Wang,Lizhuo Liu,Baicheng Pan,Zhunga Liu,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Prompt and Refinement (PAR)框架，一种面向红外小目标检测的标签噪声鲁棒非对称互学习范式，在三个数据集上取得当前最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法依赖大规模像素级精确标注，但实际场景中标注成本高、目标区分度弱，导致训练数据必然存在噪声标签，严重误导目标感知学习。因此需要一种对标签噪声鲁棒的检测方法。

Method: PAR采用非对称互学习范式：由一个预训练SAM模型和一个从头训练的ISTD专用检测器组成。两者通过同行教学（peer-teaching）协作，结合局部对比度正则化，互相利用对方预测作为监督掩码的修正线索；互补归纳偏置的交互避免标签修正退化为单模型自我确认循环。检测器预测还被用作修正性掩码提示，促进视觉基础模型的任务适应；同时引入证据不确定性估计策略以进一步缓解噪声标签的不利影响。

Result: 在三个ISTD数据集上的多种噪声标签场景下，PAR均持续达到最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: PAR通过非对称互学习、掩码提示与不确定性估计，有效应对红外小目标检测中的标签噪声问题，实现标注的渐进式修正并提升检测性能。

Abstract: Existing data-driven infrared small target detection (ISTD) methods typically require large-scale datasets with accurate pixel-level annotations for model training. However, such labor-intensive requirements are difficult to satisfy in real-world applications due to the heavy reliance on expert knowledge and the inherently weak distinctiveness of infrared small targets.
